HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_13f28fa67b48c91c2c1d","text":"No later ayah follows 105:5, so there is no later textual surprise. The retrospective surprise here comes from rereading earlier activators into the endpoint: 105:2's first جعل makes this second جعل feel like completion; 105:3's airborne scattering and 105:4's stony outpouring make عصف B002/B004 active inside the chaff image; and the basmalah's ر ح م B001 makes the final eaten-away state stand as the outside of mercy/protection rather than an isolated image of ruin.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B012","root_001020/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_21c8a671d451e458cbaf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:5:2\",\"root_001020\",[\"B003\"]],[\"105:5:3\",\"root_000043\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Başlangıçtaki ağır ve yoğun güç, özü eksilmiş, hafifçe savrulabilir kalıntıya çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_23ac73df8653b48ee7cb","text":"1. **The final `جعل` completes the earlier transformation at a new level.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `جَعَلَهُمْ` carries `ج ع ل` B002, “turning something into a state”; 105:2 uses the same branch in `يَجْعَلْ كَيْدَهُمْ فِى تَضْلِيلٍ`, and 105:1 supplies `ر ب ب` B002, nurture and completion. **Structural cue:** the prefixed `فَـ` presents this clause as consequence, and the repeated object-group suffix links the ruined actors to the owners of the earlier plan. **Assigned role:** the first `جعل` changes efficacy, the final B002 changes the condition of the agents, and `ر ب ب` B002 makes completion a feature of the governing action. **Abductive move:** the text supplies order and repeated morphology; I infer that the two transformations form one administered process. **Reading change:** the closing clause is not an isolated simile but the completion of a plan-to-planners sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001020/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3a22cda7c30b8ade805f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:5:2\",\"root_001020\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Plan için gösterilen üretken çabanın tek ürünü değersiz kuru artıkmış gibi ironik bir sonuç doğar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B004","root_000043/B007","root_001193/B001","root_001193/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3b742b1853b4f4a02307","text":"3. **The eaters/possessors become consumable prey.** Lexical evidence: ء ك ل B007, prey or animal set aside for eating; ء ك ل B004, consuming or taking wealth; ص ح ب B001/B004 in 105:1, company/possession; ف ي ل B004, elephant and its keeper/companion; ف ي ل B001, weak judgment. Structural cue: a group named by possessing/accompanying a great animal ends as \"مأكول\", passive and consumable. Assigned role: أصحاب الفيل supplies an owning/companion identity; ء ك ل B007 supplies the prey-state; ء ك ل B004 tentatively activates reversal of taking/consuming into being consumed. Abductive move: I infer reversal from controlling an animal/resource to becoming matter available to consumption; the packet supplies the ownership/company and eating branches, while I supply the reversal arrow. Reading change: the final passive adjective reads as social inversion: the powerful animal-company is rendered prey-like.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000962/B002","root_001020/B002","root_001020/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4a4ae2510981d4ddd613","text":"3. **`عَصْف` compresses destructive process into its result.** **Lexical evidence:** alongside its direct chaff sense, fixed `عَصْفٍ` activates `ع ص ف` B002, violent wind that scatters and breaks, and B004, force that sweeps a people away; contextual `ط ي ر` B002 is spreading/scattering and `س ج ل` B001 is outpouring. **Structural cue:** dispersed aerial agents and poured hard units precede the chaff comparison. **Assigned role:** B002 supplies the breakup dynamics, B004 supplies collective destruction, and B001 preserves the visible residue. **Abductive move:** no wind is stated; I infer only that the coordinated aerial volley functions like the branch’s sweeping force. **Reading change:** `كَعَصْفٍ` simultaneously presents what remains and the kind of distributed force that made it remain that way.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B001","root_000043/B006","root_000248/B002","root_001020/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_68d54706c62bce47760c","text":"1. **Final state-conversion into crop refuse.** Lexical evidence: ج ع ل B002, turning something into a state; ع ص ف B001, dry crop chaff and broken straw; ء ك ل B001, ingesting food; ء ك ل B006, being eaten away/decay. Structural cue: this is the second جعل in the surah after 105:2, now with \"هم\" as object and a simile as the resulting condition. Assigned role: جعل supplies transformation; عصف supplies the target material state; مأكول supplies consumption or eaten-away degradation. Abductive move: I infer that the final simile is the result-state of the whole sequence, not decorative comparison; the packet supplies the repeated جعل and the material branches. Reading change: the ayah reads as a completed conversion of an organized company into spent organic residue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B001","root_000043/B006","root_000043/B012","root_001020/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_737c160020288c8577fe","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:5:2\",\"root_001020\",[\"B001\"]],[\"105:5:3\",\"root_000043\",[\"B001\",\"B006\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yenmiş kalıntı benzetmesi, yalnız parçalanmış maddeyi değil, işe yarar özü tüketilmiş ve yapısı aşınmış bir son durumu anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002","root_000913/B001","root_001020/B005","root_001334/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_888ed1f52967e6e3a17e","text":"4. **Their resourceful striving yields worthless residue.** Lexical evidence: ع ص ف B005, earning and resourceful striving; ك ي د B001/B002 in 105:2, forceful handling and stratagem; ض ل ل B001, deviation from aim; ج ع ل B002, turning into state. Structural cue: the sequence moves from their intentional كيد to an عصف-state that can itself carry striving/acquisition resonance. Assigned role: كيد names the planned effort; عصف B005 names an alternate branch of busy acquisition; ضلل names the deviation of that effort's end. Abductive move: I infer ironic yield: their exertion \"produces\" only dry chaff. This is exploratory because عصف on the surface is the chaff image, while B005 is activated by the prior labor/stratagem complex. Reading change: the final ayah becomes not only annihilation but a failed harvest of their own planning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000603/B004","root_000677/B001","root_000962/B001","root_001020/B002","root_001020/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_89dd91fba05e196bc59a","text":"2. **Storm-force hidden inside the chaff image.** Lexical evidence: ع ص ف B002, violent wind that sweeps things away; ع ص ف B004, sweeping destructive force; ط ي ر B001/B002 in 105:3, airborne lightness and scattering; ر م ي B004 in 105:4, cloud/heavy-rain usage; س ج ل B001, outpouring bucket. Structural cue: the prior ayat give airborne bands and thrown-down material before the result \"عصف\". Assigned role: عصف B001 supplies the visible debris; عصف B002/B004 supplies the force that produces such debris. Abductive move: I infer a storm-like process from birds, outpouring, and projectile descent; the packet supplies several air/descent/destruction branches, while I supply the combined storm mechanism. Reading change: \"كعصف\" reads both as what they become and as the trace of the destructive force that made them so.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8c8686e7c9a78976f868","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:5:1\",\"root_000248\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Son dönüşüm, önce planın etkinliğini sonra da plan sahiplerinin durumunu değiştiren tek süreci tamamlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B001","root_001020/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a655ed8c0f75499a31fb","text":"2. **The end-state is consumed structure, not merely broken matter.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `عَصْفٍ` carries `ع ص ف` B001, dry crop chaff, husks, and broken straw; fixed `مَّأْكُولٍ` directly realizes `ء ك ل` B001, ingestion, and activates B006, being eaten away or eroded. **Structural cue:** the passive adjective modifies already fragmented crop residue and leaves the consumer unstated, emphasizing condition rather than eater. **Assigned role:** `ع ص ف` B001 supplies structural fragmentation; `ء ك ل` B001 removes what was usable; B006 extends that removal into erosion of material integrity. **Abductive move:** the packet gives a comparison, not a literal account of ingestion; I infer that consumed residue models exhaustive loss after the impacts. **Reading change:** the group is likened not simply to debris but to debris whose substance has already been used up and worn through.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001020/B003","root_001193/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_aed560cfcbaca9650ea4","text":"4. **The finale reverses mass into light, depleted residue.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `عَصْفٍ` activates `ع ص ف` B003, light and swift motion, while `مَّأْكُولٍ` activates `ء ك ل` B012, material substance or density, here under a passive form of consumption; contextual `ٱلْفِيلِ` is `ف ي ل` B004, the elephant, and `حِجَارَةٍ` is `ح ج ر` B003, hard stone. **Structural cue:** an elephant-associated formation is met by light airborne carriers of hard payloads and ends in chaff imagery. **Assigned role:** the contextual animal and stones establish concentrated weight, `ع ص ف` B003 supplies lightness, and `ء ك ل` B012 supplies the dimension of substance that the consumed condition negates. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state weights or density; I infer a material inversion from the selected objects and accepted branch images. **Reading change:** the simile measures a collapse of coherence and substance: apparent mass is converted into matter light enough to scatter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000043/B004","root_000043/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e25e6d9d3cb11c84631c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:5:3\",\"root_000043\",[\"B004\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hayvan gücünü yöneten topluluk, tüketen konumdan tüketilebilir av konumuna indirilmiş görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e582a84f3ea8603e4fc0","text":"No later ayah exists in the packet, so no later-context surprise emerged.
